Covers the full loop: writing ABAP in abapGit folder layout → /abapgit-export-zip → import in SAPGUI → fix activation errors via /abapgit-import-status-zip → repeat. No SAP-side automation; no Z* tables outside abapGit's own ZABAPGIT.
